def git(*a):return sp.check_output(['git',*a])
def g(*a):return git(*a).decode().strip()
def b(c,p):return git('show',c+':'+p)
def sha(x):return hashlib.sha256(x).hexdigest()
def ck(k,x,y):
 print(k,repr(x)[:350],'MATCH' if x==y else 'MISMATCH expected '+repr(y)[:350],flush=True)
 assert x==y,k
def pin(c,p,h):ck('SHA '+c[:7]+':'+p,sha(b(c,p)),h)
def j(c,p):return json.loads(b(c,p))
def hs(p):return sha(P.Path(p).read_bytes())
def canon(x):return sha(json.dumps(x,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=True,allow_nan=False).encode('ascii'))
